# Graph Report - L-0715  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 2106 files · ~4,281,742 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 121 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .mmd 39, (none) 37, .frozen 8)

## Summary
- 45081 nodes · 103094 edges · 1541 communities (1292 shown, 249 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 91% EXTRACTED · 9% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9642 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `8f02b5e9`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
